Maintaining UnitedNations grand strategy - abandoning now wastes diplomatic investment when Spain alliance (30 delegates) + my 13 + vassals 13 = 56 votes barely blocks Russia's 40.   - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Askia, leader of Songhai (Player 6).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Songhai
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Mandekalu Cavalry, Replacing Knight (Requires technology: Chivalry)
        - Unique Unit: Sofa, Replacing Crossbowman (Requires technology: Machinery)
        - Unique Building: Tabya, Replacing Stone Works (Requires technology: Construction)
        - Unique Building: Gumey, Replacing Caravansary (Requires technology: Currency)
        - Civilization Ability: Triple Gold from destroying Encampments and plundering Cities. Land Units gain the Amphibious and War Canoes Promotions.
      - Leader: Askia
      - PlayerID: 6
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Combined Arms: Allows you to build the Tank, a fast and deadly armor Unit.
        Completing it would unlock: Radar
      - Satellites: Allow you to build the Apollo Program, a project necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: The Internet, Lasers
      - Penicillin: All Workers receive the Fallout Reduction Promotion, which reduces the amount of damage they take while scrubbing Fallout. Allows you to build the Marine, a Unit that specializes in amphibious assaults. Also allows you to build the Medical Lab, a building which speeds the growth of your Cities.
        Completing it would unlock: Electronics
      
      ## Policies
      - Martial Law (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * No Gold Maintenance from Garrisons.
        - * +1 Happiness and +4 Culture for each Garrison.
        - * -20% Yield penalties of Puppeted Cities.
      - Peace, Land, Bread (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Peace, Land, Bread: -2 Unhappiness from Poverty and Growth increased by 20% in all Cities. Building maintenance reduced by 20%.
      - Fraternity (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +3 Food in every City.
        - * +3 Science from City Connections.
      - Workers' Faculties (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Worker Faculties: Factories increase City Science output by 10%. +100% Production towards Factories, and receive 5 Free Factories in your Empire.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Ceremony (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * +25% Production toward National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * Royal Astrologer built in Capital (+3 Science, +1 Science to all Councils, Smokehouses, and Herbalists; 1 Scientist Slot).
      - Imperium (Continuing Authority Branch):
        - * Cities gain 20 Production and Gold when their borders expand, scaling with Era (40 if all Authority policies are adopted).
        - * Receive 40 Science and Culture when you found or conquer Cities, scaling with Era. Conquest bonus also scales based on City population.
      - Hero of the People (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Hero of the People: Great Person rate increases by 25%. A Great Person of your choice appears near your Capital.
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Scientific Revolution (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Can construct Observatories (+6 Science, +1 Science from nearby Mountains, 2 Scientist Slot).
        - * +2 Science from Jungle and Snow.
      - Nobility (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Gold from Castles.
        - * +2 Gold from Armories.
        - * +100% Production towards Castles and Armories.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Dominance (Continuing Authority Branch):
        - * All Melee Units gain the Endurance Promotion (Heal 15 HP after defeating an Enemy Unit).
        - * +1 Production in every City for every 10 Military Units in Empire.
        - * +5 Military Supply.
      - Free Thought (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -5 Unhappiness from Religious Unrest in all Cities.
        - * +25% Instant Yields from Great Scientists.
        - * Earn Great Scientists +33% faster.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Foreign Service (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 100 Spy Points.
        - * Earn Great Diplomats 50% faster.
        - * +50% Rewards from City-State Quests.
        - * +1 of every Strategic Resource for every three City-State Alliances you maintain.
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 10
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 5
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 8
        - Boldness: 7
        - WarBias: 6
        - HostileBias: 7
        - WarmongerHate: 1

        - MinorCivFriendlyBias: 6
        - MinorCivNeutralBias: 6
        - MinorCivHostileBias: 9
        - MinorCivWarBias: 5
        - DenounceWillingness: 8
        - Forgiveness: 4
        - Meanness: 9
        - Neediness: 4
        - Chattiness: 7
        - DeceptiveBias: 5
        - Rationale: ENNS LOSS - MINOR CIV COMPETITION INTENSIFIES: Morocco's Great Diplomat stole Enns from Songhai influence (170 friends -> -60). While this costs 1-2 delegates, Spain remains Enns defender keeping it out of direct Russian control. Coalition likely still has 40+ vs Russia's 38 delegates. MinorCivCompetitiveness increased 7→8 reflecting intensified competition for remaining city-state delegates with only 2 turns to vote. AfraidBias increased 5→6 acknowledging Russia's 8.9x military advantage and atomic bomb capability with UN vote imminent. WarBias reduced 7→6 - maintaining offensive posture but prioritizing diplomatic blocking over conventional warfare against superior Russia/Netherlands forces. FriendlyBias reduced 4→3 - diplomatic warmth reserved for core allies (Spain) only given Morocco's betrayal. VictoryCompetitiveness 10 - maximum determination to block Russian diplomatic victory which is literal survival. Boldness 7 maintains willingness to military action when needed but within defensive framework. Hostility toward Russia (-75) and Netherlands (-100) remains maximum. Strategy: UN vote in 2 turns is existential - maximize delegate blocking through Spain alliance, defensive posture, counter-intelligence protection.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: None
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Workers' Faculties (Policy)
        - Rationale: SCIENCE ACCELERATION FOR NUCLEAR PROGRAM (7 turns to adoption) - Policy adoption timing (7 turns) partially overlaps with Nuclear Fission completion (9 turns), creating synergistic research acceleration. Workers' Faculties provides: (1) +10% Science from Factories in my 8 core cities (Gao, Tombouctu, Jenne, Taghaza, Gaziantep, Truro, Sidon, Istanbul - all have Research Labs or building them now), (2) +100% Factory production speed with 5 free Factories for newly integrated cities like Truro, Sidon, Edirne, Istanbul, (3) Immediate production boost - free factories themselves get built faster. This science boost directly accelerates reaching Nuclear Fission to build atomic deterrent and subsequent technologies. Production acceleration aids military factories infrastructure and subsequent defensive unit production. Spain alliance (29 delegates) remains primary UN blocking mechanism against Russia's 38 delegates, but nuclear deterrence is necessary contingency if Russia wins vote or breaks through diplomatic blockade.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### The Ottomans
        - Public: -6
        - Private: -6
        - Rationale: Cooling Ottomans from -10 to -6. Ottomans are a reliable vassal contributing trade routes and resources. Our forces fighting Morocco benefit from Ottoman positioning in Anatolia. -6 is appropriately cool for a subject relationship acknowledging their diminished status while still being supportive. Ottoman forces (Field Guns, Ironclads) are aiding our campaigns in Morocco/Syria region. Less hostility than warranted for independent civ recognizes their vassal utility - they provide GPT(37), resources, and military positioning.
        - UpdatedTurn: 355
        
        ### The Celts
        - Public: -50
        - Private: -50
        - Rationale: IRRELEVANT CELTIC RELATIONSHIP - BUDA DEFEATED: Celts declared war on us immediately after Russia liberated Dublin to them. However, we recaptured and puppeted Dublin the same turn. The Celts served as Russia's temporary proxy for city liberation before elimination. Their brief reign over Dublin (11 population) lasted less than a full turn as our Field Guns, Landships, Infantry and Cavalry rapidly recaptured the city, killing Russian naval units attempting to bombard it in the process. -50 max hostility acknowledges they were merely a Russian tool with no independent agency. Post-elimination relationship is irrelevant strategically as they are defeated, but recorded to document Russia's cynical manipulation tactic of liberating cities to create proxy enemies.
        - UpdatedTurn: 369
        
        ### Spain
        - Public: 18
        - Private: 18
        - Rationale: SPANISH ALLIANCE REMAINS CRITICAL: Spain continues active combat against Morocco - Paratroopers engaging Moroccan Machine Guns, Commandos attacking forts near Marrakech this turn. Spain's 23 UN delegates (53% victory) are essential to blocking Russia's 88% UN victory. Defensive Pact (13 turns remaining) and Declaration of Friendship remain our most valuable diplomatic assets. Spain's military production (Landships, Paratroopers upgrading) demonstrates commitment to shared anti-Russia/Morocco strategy. Maintaining warmth at 18 acknowledges their sacrifices and leadership against Morocco while we rebuild from 2 Dreadnought losses. No changes needed - alliance remains strongest counter to Russian diplomatic dominance.
        - UpdatedTurn: 381
        
        ### Russia
        - Public: -75
        - Private: -75
        - Rationale: MAXIMUM HOSTILITY INCREASED: Russia CAPTURED BURSA this turn and BUILT AN ATOMIC BOMB. This is catastrophic escalation. Bursa was my coastal puppet (10 population) with Great Barrier Reef, Atoll, Marble, Iron, Academy, Holy Site. Russia now controls these strategic tiles. Russia's Atomic Bomb (ID 17078) creates existential nuclear threat. Russia has 12 Uranium vs my 5, 20895 military strength vs my 4140, 79% UN victory. NO谈判选项 - Russia is committed to victory through nuclear + diplomatic dominance. Hostility increased from -50/-50 to -75/-75 (maximum allowed) because this is now a fight for survival. Must coordinate with Spain, potentially Netherlands and Morocco to prevent Russian UN victory and nuclear usage. Every diplomatic action must be colored by Russia's nuclear threat and territorial aggression.
        - UpdatedTurn: 391
        
        ### The Aztecs
        - Public: -10
        - Private: -5
        - Rationale: Netherlands diplomatic flexibility maintained after turn 394 adjustments. Netherlands Ambassador entered Moscow (Event 87) indicating diplomatic communication channels remain open despite recent destroyer/Ironclad combat. With UN vote in 2 turns on Russia's World Ideology proposal, maintaining Netherlands at -10/-5 (vs earlier -15/-10) preserves diplomatic flexibility. Their 14 delegates could be critical if Netherlands recognizes Russia's nuclear threat (Atomic Bomb + Bursa capture). Full hostility would push Netherlands completely into Russian camp; moderate -10/-5 acknowledges recent tensions while leaving room for potential alignment shift against Russia's existential threat.
        - UpdatedTurn: 397
        
        ### Morocco
        - Public: -30
        - Private: -30
        - Rationale: REITERATING MOROCCAN HOSTILITY (no change): Morocco's 13 UN delegates actively support Russia's 73% victory. Morocco recently plundered Spain's trade route (600 gold) and submitted Anti-Spain sanctions. Spain (my ally) continues active combat against Morocco - Paratroopers engaging Moroccan Machine Guns near Marrakech this turn. Morocco shares Order ideology with me but has definitively chosen Russia over cooperation. -30 hostility signals Morocco as adversarial priority after Russia. Let Spain continue weakening Morocco militarily - my diplomatic hostility reflects Morocco's proxy support of Russia without directly engaging Moroccan forces while Spain handles the conflict.
        - UpdatedTurn: 399
        
        ### The Netherlands
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: NETHERLANDS WAR DECLARATION: Netherlands formally declared war on me this turn, joining Russia as active military enemy. Netherlands has 22 UN delegates, military strength 4800, controls 4 city-states (including Aksum, Sokoto, Enns, Yamatai) that also declared war, and is allied with Russia. Their declaration transforms a diplomatic flexibility concern into direct military threat. Relationship adjusted from -10/-5 to -100/-100 (maximum hostility) to reflect active war status. Loss of potential Netherlands' 22 delegates votes is offset by making Netherlands a Russian-aligned target rather than a voting bloc. Will fight Netherlands defensively while maintaining primary focus on Russia's nuclear/UN threat.
        - UpdatedTurn: 400
        
        ### Yamatai
        - Public: -50
        - Private: -50
        - Rationale: YAMATAI DECLARED WAR AGAIN (turn 402) - This is the THIRD time Yamatai cycles between war and peace (friends → war → friends → war). Yamatai is clearly serving as Russia's proxy to drain my military resources and diplomatic focus. With Russia at 83% diplomatic victory (6 turns away), I cannot waste military units on rebellious city-states that will immediately declare war again. However, must maintain diplomatic hostility (-50/-50) to reflect Yamatai's untrustworthy behavior and Russian alignment without diverting forces. Peace treaties are meaningless against a city-state that immediately redeclares war. Limited retaliation only when Yamatai units threaten core cities - primary military focus must remain on blocking Russian UN victory through Spain alliance and diplomatic maneuvering.
        - UpdatedTurn: 404
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: DEFENSIVE POSTURE WITH DIPLOMATIC VIGILANCE: Maintaining current strategic configuration as it remains optimal for the critical UN vote situation. Russia's 35 delegates vs our coalition of 43 (13+30 Spain) keeps blocking advantage intact, but Russia's massive military advantage (41,936 vs 4,703) and Nuclear Fission 5 turns away demand caution. Recent losses: Dreadnought destroyed by Russian Modern Armor, Dreadnought lost to Russian-aligned city-state, Cavalry killed by Russian B17 bomber. However, defensive lines remain strong: Gao (5,990), Truro (5,692), Tombouctu (4,775), Jenne (4,764), Gaziantep (3,238), Sidon (3,717), Istanbul (2,888) - all with friendly dominance. Military successfully destroyed Netherlands Dreadnought near Salem. Nuclear Fission research in 5 turns provides critical deterrent. Workers' Faculties policy (4 turns) will boost factory science across 8 core cities. Spain alliance (30 delegates) remains vital blockade against Russian UN victory. No strategic changes needed - current defense-first, diplomacy-focused approach balances existential diplomatic threat with military realities.
        - GrandStrategy: UnitedNations

        ## DiplomaticVictory
        - VotesNeeded: 42
        - Status: World Congress - Voting in 10 turns
        - ActiveResolutions:
          - Natural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each Natural Wonder provides +2 to all Yields when worked.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Spanish Sphere of Influence: Wellington:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Russian Sphere of Influence: Ban Chiang:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - World Science Initiative:
            - Description: Increases up to 33% generation of Great Scientists, Engineers, and Merchants. Decreases up to 33% generation of Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians.
              Civilizations with Scores from Technologies and Future Tech below the global median will receive a boost to Food and Science up to 33%.
              Details: The boosts are proportional to the difference between the score of the Civilization and the leader.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Cultural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each World Wonder provides +2 Culture to its city, and each Great Work provides +1 Science to its city.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Spanish Sphere of Influence: Salem:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Historical Landmarks:
            - Description: Great Person tile improvements provide +1 to Food, Production, and Gold when worked, and Landmarks built by Archaeologists provide +1 Science, Culture, and Faith when worked.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Archaeology.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Russian Sphere of Influence: Vancouver:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Scholars In Residence:
            - Description: Civilizations have -20% Research cost for Technologies already discovered by another Civilization. Additional -2% for every City-State Ally, up to -50%.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - World Ideology: Freedom:
            - Description: Designate an Ideology as the official World Ideology. Civilizations following the Ideology get an additional Delegate, plus 1 Delegate for every other Civilization that follows the Ideology. Public opinion in favor of that Ideology is increased for all Civilizations. Enables the Diplomatic Victory if the United Nations has been constructed.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Nuclear Fission.
            - EnactedOn: 398
          - Global Peace Accords:
            - Description: Unit maintenance costs are raised by 25% Gold. 2.0x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 0.5x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Spanish Sphere of Influence: Bunkeya:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Russian Sphere of Influence: Palatium:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - World Religion: Buddhism:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.
            - EnactedOn: -1
        - Proposals:
          - Enact: Passport System:
            - Description: Increases international travel between Civilizations. Increases the Tourism modifier for all Civilizations by 50%.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Flight.
            - Proposer: Spain

- replay:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Askia, leader of Songhai (Player 6).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Songhai
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Mandekalu Cavalry, Replacing Knight (Requires technology: Chivalry)
        - Unique Unit: Sofa, Replacing Crossbowman (Requires technology: Machinery)
        - Unique Building: Tabya, Replacing Stone Works (Requires technology: Construction)
        - Unique Building: Gumey, Replacing Caravansary (Requires technology: Currency)
        - Civilization Ability: Triple Gold from destroying Encampments and plundering Cities. Land Units gain the Amphibious and War Canoes Promotions.
      - Leader: Askia
      - PlayerID: 6
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Combined Arms: Allows you to build the Tank, a fast and deadly armor Unit.
        Completing it would unlock: Radar
      - Satellites: Allow you to build the Apollo Program, a project necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: The Internet, Lasers
      - Penicillin: All Workers receive the Fallout Reduction Promotion, which reduces the amount of damage they take while scrubbing Fallout. Allows you to build the Marine, a Unit that specializes in amphibious assaults. Also allows you to build the Medical Lab, a building which speeds the growth of your Cities.
        Completing it would unlock: Electronics
      
      ## Policies
      - Martial Law (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * No Gold Maintenance from Garrisons.
        - * +1 Happiness and +4 Culture for each Garrison.
        - * -20% Yield penalties of Puppeted Cities.
      - Peace, Land, Bread (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Peace, Land, Bread: -2 Unhappiness from Poverty and Growth increased by 20% in all Cities. Building maintenance reduced by 20%.
      - Fraternity (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +3 Food in every City.
        - * +3 Science from City Connections.
      - Workers' Faculties (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Worker Faculties: Factories increase City Science output by 10%. +100% Production towards Factories, and receive 5 Free Factories in your Empire.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Ceremony (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * +25% Production toward National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * Royal Astrologer built in Capital (+3 Science, +1 Science to all Councils, Smokehouses, and Herbalists; 1 Scientist Slot).
      - Imperium (Continuing Authority Branch):
        - * Cities gain 20 Production and Gold when their borders expand, scaling with Era (40 if all Authority policies are adopted).
        - * Receive 40 Science and Culture when you found or conquer Cities, scaling with Era. Conquest bonus also scales based on City population.
      - Hero of the People (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Hero of the People: Great Person rate increases by 25%. A Great Person of your choice appears near your Capital.
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Scientific Revolution (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Can construct Observatories (+6 Science, +1 Science from nearby Mountains, 2 Scientist Slot).
        - * +2 Science from Jungle and Snow.
      - Nobility (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Gold from Castles.
        - * +2 Gold from Armories.
        - * +100% Production towards Castles and Armories.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Dominance (Continuing Authority Branch):
        - * All Melee Units gain the Endurance Promotion (Heal 15 HP after defeating an Enemy Unit).
        - * +1 Production in every City for every 10 Military Units in Empire.
        - * +5 Military Supply.
      - Free Thought (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -5 Unhappiness from Religious Unrest in all Cities.
        - * +25% Instant Yields from Great Scientists.
        - * Earn Great Scientists +33% faster.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Foreign Service (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 100 Spy Points.
        - * Earn Great Diplomats 50% faster.
        - * +50% Rewards from City-State Quests.
        - * +1 of every Strategic Resource for every three City-State Alliances you maintain.
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - decisions:
    - #0:
      - toolName: set-research
      - args:
        - Technology: Combined Arms
        - Rationale: Unlocks Radar to establish air and naval superiority, countering Russia's advanced bombers and protecting our territory and naval task forces.
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Rationale: Boosts science by +10% in all cities with factories, accelerating our research of Nuclear Fission and Combined Arms, and provides substantial factory production bonuses.
        - Policy: Workers' Faculties
    - #2:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Rationale: Maximize cooperation and trade with Spain, our critical defensive pact ally, ensuring a unified voting block in the upcoming UN World Leader election in 10 turns.
        - Private: 30
        - Public: 30
        - TargetID: 4
    - #3:
      - toolName: set-relationship

        - Rationale: Maintain warm relations with our voluntary vassal to secure their absolute loyalty, trade benefits, and 5 crucial UN votes.
        - Private: 15
        - Public: 15
        - TargetID: 5
    - #4:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Private: 10
        - Rationale: Secure the loyalty of our capitulated vassal, keeping their 8 UN votes aligned with our coalition and optimizing our Anatolian military coordination.
        - TargetID: 1
        - Public: 10
    - #5:
      - toolName: set-flavors

        - Flavors:
          - Offense: 55
          - Nuke: 95
          - Naval: 75
          - UseNuke: 35
          - Diplomacy: 100
          - Defense: 85
          - Espionage: 95
          - Mobilization: 75
          - Science: 100
          - Gold: 75
          - Production: 90
        - GrandStrategy: UnitedNations
      - rationale: Slightly increase Offense and Naval focus to coordinate the massive Songhai-Ottoman fleet (15 modern ships) in capturing Russia's allied City-States, Vancouver and Palatium, stripping them of delegates before the UN vote. Lower UseNuke to 35 to ensure a retaliatory-only ethical posture that minimizes catastrophic civilian and environmental harm while maintaining a strong strategic nuclear deterrent. Raise Mobilization and Production to build deterrents and reinforcements quickly.

  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Defining Current Objectives**
            
            I'm currently focused on understanding our immediate goals for this turn. My priority is to analyze the context of Turn 410 and determine if our immediate priorities involve setting technologies, policies, our persona, relationships, or flavors.
            
            **Clarifying Policy Options**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our policy choices, considering options like Martial Law or Fraternity. I need to confirm the exact timing for setting the next policy to make the most strategic decision.
            
            **Analyzing Policy and Research**
            
            I'm reviewing our policy options, specifically regarding "Workers' Faculties" set for the next two turns. I need to confirm if setting it again is beneficial or if we should focus on the available technologies: Combined Arms, Satellites, or Penicillin.
            
            **Assessing Diplomatic Threats**
            
            I'm reassessing Russia's imminent diplomatic victory, noting their delegate count and the impact of World Ideology. With only 10 turns until the World Congress, I'm scrutinizing their lead and potential actions by other civs.
            
            **Reviewing Diplomatic Bonds**
            
            I'm updating my understanding of our diplomatic standings, noting current friendships and alliances. Salem's relationship with Spain appears complex, influenced by an enacted Sphere of Influence, which I'm now factoring into my strategic assessment of our influence with both powers.
            
            **Evaluating Diplomatic Alignments**
            
            I'm dissecting current alliances and rivalries, focusing on Spain's connections. I'm clarifying Yamatai's status with Spain and investigating the absence of a declared war between Spain and Russia, despite their Defensive Pact and Russia's conflict with Songhai.
            
            **Investigating Diplomatic Logic**
            
            I'm untangling a recent puzzle regarding defensive pacts and war declarations. It appears Spain and Russia are not at war, despite my initial assumptions based on Spain's defensive pact with Songhai. I'm now verifying the explicit relationship statuses between these powers to understand the nuances of their current diplomatic stances and any potential omissions in my understanding of the conflict dynamics.
            
            **Analyzing Nuclear Deterrence**
            
            I'm now prioritizing Russia's atomic capabilities and our uranium reserves. I'm evaluating the strategic advantage of completing Nuclear Fission for defensive atomic bomb production against potential Russian threats, and concurrently assessing the optimal technology choice following this research from the options of Combined Arms, Satellites, or Penicillin.
            
            **Prioritizing Defensive Technologies**
            
            I'm reconsidering our research path. Given Russia's overwhelming military and technological lead, achieving a Science Victory via Satellites seems improbable. My current focus is on Combined Arms for Radar, which unlocks crucial defensive and offensive air capabilities needed to counter their B17s and Jet Fighters, essential for survival.
            
            **Selecting Research Direction**
            
            I am focusing on the immediate research path, concluding that Combined Arms to unlock Radar is the most pressing need, rather than pursuing Penicillin or a remote Science Victory. My analysis confirms that this will grant us vital defensive and offensive air capabilities to counter Russia's advanced units, ensuring our survival.
            
            **Selecting Optimal Policy**
            
            I've finalized my decision on the best policy to implement, confirming that Workers' Faculties offers the most significant boost to our science generation. This acceleration is critical for achieving Nuclear Fission and Radar research, providing the necessary technological edge for survival.
            
            **Adjusting Diplomatic Priorities**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our diplomatic standings, particularly with Yamatai. It appears my previous assessment of Yamatai as hostile was outdated; they are currently friendly and allied with Spain. I'm prioritizing reinforcing our alliance with Spain and analyzing Morocco's complex war engagements to gauge any shifts in regional stability or opportunities.
            
            **Analyzing Diplomatic Paradoxes**
            
            I'm grappling with a perplexing contradiction: Morocco is at war with Russia and the Netherlands, serving as a vital shield, yet we are hostile towards them and allied with Spain, who also denounces Morocco. This intricate web of alliances and enmities requires careful navigation to maintain our strategic position.
            
            **Analyzing Delegate Mechanics**
            
            I am dissecting how delegates are allocated, specifically to understand how Russia accrues their 40 votes. My focus is on their permanent city-state alliances and how vassal states like the Netherlands might influence voting outcomes in the World Congress.
            
            **Calculating Delegate Strength**
            
            I've calculated the delegate counts for various blocks. Russia and its vassal the Netherlands hold 58 votes, while our block, including Spain and Songhai, holds 56. Morocco, with 13 votes, is the crucial swing player in the upcoming World Congress.
            
            **Assessing Diplomatic Victory**
            
            I'm analyzing Russia's path to diplomatic victory. They're close to the 42 votes needed, potentially by allying with another city-state. My immediate focus is on how to prevent them from securing those additional votes to maintain our position.
            
            **Disrupting Russian Delegation**
            
            I'm leveraging our spies to counter Russia's delegate advantage. While permanent Spheres of Influence are a barrier, I'm investigating options to disrupt their potential city-state flips, particularly Aksum and Sokoto. Rigging elections in Enns or potentially influencing Yamatai are active considerations.
            
            **Leveraging Diplomatic Assets**
            
            I've just confirmed that expending our Great Diplomat in Yamatai successfully boosted our influence to 599, solidifying our Friendship and aiding Spain in securing Yamatai against Russian influence. This strategic move directly impacts our diplomatic block's strength in the World Congress.
            
            **Securing City-State Support**
            
            I've successfully leveraged our Great Diplomat to secure Yamatai's support, boosting our influence to 599 and ensuring their friendship. This is crucial for our diplomatic bloc and directly counters Russia's attempts to gain influence there.
            
            **Adjusting Resource Flavors**
            
            I'm prioritizing military build-up and atomic bomb production, thus increasing Mobilization and Production flavors. My Gold flavor is also elevated to sustain unit purchases and diplomatic actions. Espionage flavor is boosted to support counter-intelligence efforts.
            
            **Refining Grand Strategy**
            
            My thoughts are now focused on confirming our overarching strategy. Given Russia's overwhelming diplomatic lead, I've decided that the United Nations grand strategy is indeed the optimal choice, as it prioritizes city-state alliances and gold generation, which are crucial for blocking their victory.
            
            **Considering Ethical Nuances**
            
            I'm now contemplating the ethical implications of nuclear weapons, acknowledging their catastrophic potential. Since Russia already possesses them, I'm focusing on acquiring them for deterrence rather than offensive use, ensuring responsible management of this powerful technology.
            
            **Adjusting Nuke Flavors**
            
            I'm refining our nuclear posture. To align with ethical concerns while maintaining deterrence, I'm lowering the `UseNuke` flavor to 35 to reflect a retaliatory-only stance, keeping `Nuke` at 95 for a strong deterrent against Russia.
            
            **Reviewing Persona Settings**
            
            I'm confirming our current persona values remain optimal. The recent adjustments to minor civ competitiveness and fear reflect our current strategic realities well. No significant shifts are immediately necessary.
            
            **Strengthening Spanish Alliance**
            
            I'm reinforcing our critical alliance with Spain, increasing our relationship values to Public 30, Private 30. This maximizes mutual cooperation, vital for blocking Russia's diplomatic victory, and will be enacted. I also noted the Aztecs are our voluntary vassal, a status that requires no immediate adjustment.
            
            **Strengthening Vassal Bonds**
            
            I'm now focused on reinforcing our relationship with the Aztecs. Despite being our voluntary vassal, negative modifiers are affecting our opinion of them. I'm rectifying this by setting our relationship to Public 15 and Private 15 to ensure their continued loyalty and World Congress votes.
            
            **Warming Vassal Relations**
            
            I'm now working on solidifying our relationships with our vassals, the Ottomans and Aztecs. Improving our standing with them ensures their continued loyalty and crucial UN votes, essential for our bloc. I'm also re-evaluating our stance with Morocco, as their negative modifiers require careful consideration.
            
            **Analyzing Diplomatic Voting Dynamics**
            
            I'm recalculating Russia's potential delegate strength, realizing Morocco's votes won't go to them due to our ongoing war. This significantly alters their potential victory margin, shifting the focus to how Morocco's delegates will actually be cast in the World Congress.
            
            **Analyzing World Leader Vote**
            
            I'm re-evaluating the World Leader vote. Russia has 58 delegates, Spain has 56. Crucially, Morocco, at war with Russia, will not vote for them. If Morocco votes for our ally Spain, they win with 69 votes. This could secure a shared victory.
            
            **Assessing Vancouver's Threat**
            
            I'm evaluating the possibility of capturing Vancouver to deny Russia its delegates. While the city-state has high defense and is guarded by enemy naval units, I'm assessing if this direct action is a viable strategy to prevent Russia's diplomatic victory or reduce their delegate count below the threshold.
            
            **Investigating City-State Manipulation**
            
            I'm delving into the intricacies of city-state influence and Russian control, specifically questioning the feasibility of flipping Aksum due to its alliance with Russia's vassal, the Netherlands. My thoughts are now on whether we can conquer these key city-states instead, as Russia's permanent Spheres of Influence prevent diplomatic coups.
            
            **Assessing Morocco's Allegiance**
            
            I'm grappling with Morocco's voting intentions. They despise Russia and Spain, meaning their 13 delegates are unpredictable. I'm now focusing on whether we can secure their vote, as they won't back Russia due to the ongoing war, and Spain is also hostile to them.
            
            **Capturing Vancouver**
            
            I'm now assessing the feasibility of capturing Vancouver. We're already at war with them, and their proximity is favorable. I'm verifying if our naval presence, including Dreadnoughts, is sufficient to overcome their defenses and if taking this city-state is a viable strategy to deny Russia crucial delegates and prevent their diplomatic victory.
            
            **Securing Vancouver Attack**
            
            I'm focusing on a decisive maneuver: capturing Vancouver. Our combined Songhai-Ottoman naval fleet, comprising 3 Dreadnoughts, 10 Destroyers, and 1 Submarine, is perfectly positioned near the city-state. This powerful force is more than capable of overcoming Vancouver's defenses within the crucial next 10 turns, directly denying Russia vital delegates.
            
            **Conquering City-States**
            
            I'm now focusing on the critical tactic of capturing Vancouver, and potentially Palatium. Denying Russia these city-state delegates is paramount to preventing their diplomatic victory. Our formidable naval fleet is well-positioned for this crucial maneuver within the next ten turns.
            
            **Executing Naval Offensive**
            
            I am finalizing the plan to capture Vancouver and Palatium, shifting our flavor focus to support this decisive naval action. My priority now is configuring our offensive and naval flavors to ensure the success of this maneuver and secure our victory.
            
            **Shifting Focus Strategically**
            
            I'm refining our strategy to prioritize tactical military actions that support our diplomatic goals. Keeping the "UnitedNations" grand strategy is key, but I'm adjusting our flavors to emphasize offense and naval power to execute critical city-state captures, securing our victory.
            
            **Executing Naval Offensive**
            
            My current focus is on the decisive capture of Vancouver and Palatium. Denying Russia these city-state delegates is paramount to preventing their diplomatic victory. Our formidable naval fleet is well-positioned for this crucial maneuver within the next ten turns, aligning our flavors to emphasize offense and naval power for success.
            
            **Executing Naval Offensive**
            
            I'm finalizing the plan to capture Vancouver and Palatium. Denying Russia these city-state delegates is paramount to preventing their diplomatic victory. Our formidable naval fleet is well-positioned for this crucial maneuver within the next ten turns, aligning our flavors to emphasize offense and naval power for success.
            
            **Confirming Tool Parameters**
            
            I am meticulously verifying the parameters for the `set-relationship` tool across Spain, the Ottomans, and the Aztecs. I've confirmed the correct Player IDs for each of these entities to ensure our diplomatic actions are precisely targeted for optimal World Congress outcomes.
            
            **Executing Naval Offensive**
            
            My current focus is on the decisive capture of Vancouver and Palatium. Denying Russia these city-state delegates is paramount to preventing their diplomatic victory. Our formidable naval fleet is well-positioned for this crucial maneuver within the next ten turns, aligning our flavors to emphasize offense and naval power for success.
            
            **Executing Naval Offensive**
